FARB 2025 OCCUPATIONAL JOB CATALOGUE
Classification: Internal Workforce Structure
Workforce Reference: 29,000 employees
Departments: 100
Distinct Job Titles: 2,000
Record Format: Job Code | Department | Job Title | Role Summary
Note: This catalogue describes occupational roles available within FARB. It does not assign positions to named employees or indicate how many posts are filled.
